## driver-fernando-alonso#3

- Dokumentum: Fernando Alonso

Entitások:

- `e1` Fernando Alonso (Driver): A Formula One driver who won his maiden title after winning seven Grands Prix in 2005.
- `e2` Renault (Team): A Formula One team that Alonso joined as a test driver before becoming a full-time driver.
- `e3` 2005 (Season): The season in which Alonso won his maiden title.
- `e4` Malaysian Grand Prix (GrandPrix): A race where Alonso became the then-youngest polesitter.
- `e5` Hungarian Grand Prix (GrandPrix): A race where Alonso became the then-youngest race winner.
- `e6` World Drivers' Champion (Title): The championship title won by Alonso in 2005.

Kapcsolatok:

- `e1` -DROVE_FOR-> `e2`: Alonso joined Renault as a test driver before becoming a full-time driver.
- `e1` -WON-> `e6`: Alonso won his maiden title in 2005.
- `e6` -AWARDED_IN-> `e3`: The title won by Alonso belongs to the 2005 season.
- `e4` -ROUND_OF-> `e3`: The Malaysian Grand Prix is a round of the 2005 season.
- `e5` -ROUND_OF-> `e3`: The Hungarian Grand Prix is a round of the 2005 season.

## driver-fernando-alonso#8

- Dokumentum: Fernando Alonso

Entitások:

- `e1` Fernando Alonso (Driver): Alonso has achieved 32 race wins, 22 pole positions, 26 fastest laps, and 106 podiums in Formula One. He is contracted to remain at Aston Martin until at least the end of the 2027 season.
- `e2` Aston Martin (Team): Alonso is contracted to remain at Aston Martin until at least the end of the 2027 season.
- `e3` 2026 (Season): As of the 2026 Bahrain Grand Prix.
- `e4` Bahrain Grand Prix (GrandPrix): As of the 2026 Bahrain Grand Prix.

- `e4` -ROUND_OF-> `e3`: The Bahrain Grand Prix is a round of the 2026 season.

- `e3` Championship of the Asturias (Title): A children's junior championship won by Alonso in 1988 and 1989.
- `e4` Championship of Galicia (Title): A children's junior championship won by Alonso in 1988 and 1989.
- `e5` Genís Marcó (Driver): Go-kart importer who mentored Alonso.
- `e6` José Luis Echevarria (Driver): Kart track owner who told Genís Marcó about Alonso.

## reference-list-of-formula-one-world-drivers-champions#1

- Dokumentum: List of Formula One World Drivers' Champions

Entitások:

- `e1` Lewis Hamilton (Driver): British driver with 7 world titles.
- `e2` Max Verstappen (Driver): Dutch driver with 4 world titles.
- `e3` Sebastian Vettel (Driver): German driver with 4 world titles.
- `e4` Fernando Alonso (Driver): Spanish driver with 2 world titles.
- `e5` Michael Schumacher (Driver): German driver with 7 world titles.
- `e6` Kimi Räikkönen (Driver): Finnish driver with 1 world title.
- `e7` Nico Rosberg (Driver): German driver with 1 world title.
- `e8` Jenson Button (Driver): British driver with 1 world title.
- `e9` McLaren (Team): Constructor associated with Lewis Hamilton, Fernando Alonso, and Kimi Räikkönen.
- `e10` Mercedes (Team): Constructor associated with Lewis Hamilton and Nico Rosberg.
- `e11` Red Bull Racing (Team): Constructor associated with Max Verstappen and Sebastian Vettel.
- `e12` Renault (Team): Constructor associated with Fernando Alonso.
- `e13` Ferrari (Team): Constructor associated with Sebastian Vettel and Kimi Räikkönen.
- `e14` Williams (Team): Constructor associated with Nico Rosberg.
- `e15` BAR/Honda (Team): Constructor associated with Jenson Button.
- `e16` Brawn (Team): Constructor associated with Jenson Button.

- `e4` -DROVE_FOR-> `e9`: Fernando Alonso drove for McLaren.
- `e5` -DROVE_FOR-> `e9`: Michael Schumacher drove for McLaren.
- `e5` -DROVE_FOR-> `e13`: Michael Schumacher drove for Ferrari.
- `e6` -DROVE_FOR-> `e9`: Kimi Räikkönen drove for McLaren.
- `e6` -DROVE_FOR-> `e13`: Kimi Räikkönen drove for Ferrari.
- `e7` -DROVE_FOR-> `e14`: Nico Rosberg drove for Williams.
- `e7` -DROVE_FOR-> `e10`: Nico Rosberg drove for Mercedes.
- `e8` -DROVE_FOR-> `e15`: Jenson Button drove for BAR/Honda.

- `e2` George Kurtz (Driver): CEO and founder of cybersecurity firm CrowdStrike, co-owner of the team.
- `e3` Mercedes-Benz (Team): Formula One team.
- `e4` Ola Källenius (Driver): Chairman of Mercedes-Benz.
- `e5` Sir Jim Ratcliffe (Driver): Chairman of INEOS.

Kapcsolatok:

- `e1` -DROVE_FOR-> `e3` role=team principal: Wolff remains team principal and CEO of Mercedes-Benz.
- `e2` -DROVE_FOR-> `e3` [2025–None] role=co-owner: Kurtz became a co-owner of the team.
